# ViralVideoPrompts

> **A curated directory of viral AI video prompts, creative video ideas, and AI-generated video examples.**
> Discover prompts for Seedance, Veo, Kling, Sora, Runway, and more.

This repository is a GitHub-mirrored **catalog** of every tutorial published under [`videosprompt.org/creative`](https://videosprompt.org/creative). Each entry links back to the original source.

🌐 **Live site:** [videosprompt.org](https://videosprompt.org/) · **Creative section:** [videosprompt.org/creative](https://videosprompt.org/creative) · ➕ [Submit a prompt](https://videosprompt.org/submit)

## 📄 About

This repo is an **index/catalog**. Every entry points back to its canonical source on [videosprompt.org](https://videosprompt.org/). The full prompt text, adaptation examples, and FAQ live at the linked URLs — this repo is for discoverability, SEO, and offline browsing.

**Prompt origin:** All prompts in this directory originate from **[socialtoprompt.com](https://socialtoprompt.com/)** and are mirrored to [videosprompt.org/creative](https://videosprompt.org/creative) for the community.
